PURPOSE

RETRIEVAL RULE
Treat explicit platform labels, verification/status fields, source labels, and warnings as part of the fact. Never silently remove them.

SOURCE POLICY:
- never_guess_drop_rates: true
- fraction_is_primary: true
- percentage_is_derived_only_when_fraction_exists: true
- if_conflict_show_both: true
- platform_separation_required: true
- BB/Ephinea routes are not silently treated as vanilla Blue Burst.
- GameCube and Xbox are grouped only where the source basis treats them as Version 3 family.
- needs_verification means the route target is strategically relevant but the exact fraction is not directly machine-verified in the current source slice.

Verification states:
- verified
- verified_for_gc_xbox
- verified_for_blue_burst
- bb_ephinea_only
- community_synthesis
- conflicting_source_values
- needs_verification
